FEAT335-ACQUISITION-AUDIT-01 — exhaustive acquisition/initial-state review

We are continuing the owner's complete CRPG system specification, retaining the twelve-character expedition, parallel activities and host permissions. Software conversion and human balance testing are later. Actual final joint whole-game flow agreement precedes the already-authorized commit/push, verified remote, careful cosmology review and preservation-based cleanup. Do not reopen completed crafting/language/onboarding/EX58 or ACQ01–05 absent a concrete mismatch.

ACQ05 is fulfilled locally: seven replacements in five files, 272 independent descendant checks and 206 lifecycle checks across all20 named groups/71 traces. The preserved original and old completion-first model remain historical. Subsequent paid-selection passes cover Prodigy; three trade feats; eight half-feats; five fixed martial/metamagic/elemental menus; five skill/tool/language menus; eleven casting choices; and Weapon Focus/Mastery. The latest weapon pass has591 checks/47 histories, all40 reviewed equipment profiles, one actual Monk unarmed Focus witness and two explicitly conditional Troll Claws witnesses. These are limited local reports, not your approval or335 complete acquisitions.

Please inspect the attached current full335 acquisition contracts, complete public/internal candidates, shared rules, full supplements, exact spell dependencies, class/ancestry/equipment sources and complete local reports. PACKAGE_MANIFEST.json binds every byte. START_HERE.json indexes report scopes and historical source-binding differences; those differences must not be mistaken for executed checks against newer source revisions. The 335-row source index supplies navigation, not semantic acceptance. Compare the COMPLETE operative procedures, not keyword hits or only normalized prerequisite trees.

Requested deliverable: one finite, row-by-row acquisition/initial-state audit covering all335 exact IDs. This is a targeted readiness audit of FXC05 and acquisition portions of FXC04, not the final assembled full-feat verdict and not a new generic source-authoring conference.

For EACH ID identify: (a) actual printed/operative acquisition predicates and repeatability; (b) every mandatory permanent selection and fixed snapshot branch, with its real menu/source; (c) initial finite allowances, pools/reserves/empty containers, shared parent-pool behavior, capacity-only changes, ownership without body/item/stock, and any acquisition that must not initialize until a later event; (d) deferred/unavailable or external-source-conditional branches; (e) which attached evidence actually covers each obligation and what remains untested or unestablished. A feat with no new selection/resource still needs an explicit source-based disposition, not omission. Shared-rule-backed groups may avoid redundant work but must enumerate their exact members and per-feat exceptions. Keep use-time costs, clocks, damage and real physical premises distinct from acquisition.

Return machine-readable rows plus a concise finite remaining-work list grouped by actual missing obligation. Do not infer full acquisition completion by adding partial coverage counts. Do not demand actual campaign records merely to exercise already-approved conditional interfaces; equally do not promote a fixture to a real credential. Identify contradictions in the new local models if any. Proposed tests should cover real distinct boundaries and interactions, not repeat thousands of tautological checks. Preserve source permissions rather than reducing the menu to the easiest supported witness.

One concrete classification question needs your source comparison: Weapon Focus expressly admits a specific unarmed profile with an actual proficiency receipt. Weapon Mastery names an actual proficient weapon profile and a specific genuine natural weapon, rejecting umbrella labels. The retained source table places Unarmed strike under Simple melee weapons; reviewed equipment says unarmed is an attack option rather than an inventory object, and the40 physical equipment profiles exclude it. The local pass proves Monk's Simple-training route only for specific unarmed Focus; it leaves specific-unarmed Mastery outside the slice, not illegal. Determine the exact current disposition for a specific proficient ordinary/Monk unarmed profile under Mastery, and whether Troll Claws' express natural-weapon/unarmed wording plus an actual existing training grant establishes exact-profile proficiency or still needs a separate external credential. Do not import outside-game defaults. Cite actual clauses; if a genuinely NEW minimal completion is required, give exact guarded changes, not silent interpretation. Keep unprovided genuine natural profiles as source-qualified possibilities, not a blanket ban or a free proficiency grant.

Likewise check the distinction that Focus explicitly requires ongoing operative proficiency, while Mastery specifies proficiency at acquisition and its own profile/level use predicates. Do not add an ongoing gate absent a controlling clause. Preserve the level6 gate and actual earned opportunities, nonrepeatable Mastery, broadest critical range, natural19 needingAC, and natural20 automatic hit.

Prefer KEEP where complete source already controls. If an ambiguity is found elsewhere, cite the exact current identity/field and propose only its necessary completion. Any new authoring is distinct from your audit conclusions, and no broader candidate promotion follows. Return full source-linked JSON/Markdown and any actual reproduction checker/results in a downloadable ZIP. Mark calculations you actually executed separately from authored expectations. Keep all original objects, historical evidence, status/selection flags, the two null deferred payloads, and every unrelated source unchanged. No cosmology interpretation, deletion, runtime selection, full-game approval or extra owner confirmation is requested.

from pathlib import Path
import json, hashlib, collections, re
W=Path('/mnt/data/feat335_acquisition_audit_work')
R=W/'submission'
def load(rel): return json.loads((R/rel).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
base="DescentReviewChanges/ReviewBible/"
pb=base+"data/public/feat_candidates/exact_01/"
db=base+"data/director/feat_candidates/exact_01/"
audit="docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/"
contracts=load(audit+"FEAT_EXACT_01_ACQUISITION_CONTRACTS.json")
public=load(pb+"candidate.json")
internal=load(db+"candidate.json")
start=load(audit+"feat335-acquisition-audit-01-input/START_HERE.json")
manifest=load("PACKAGE_MANIFEST.json")
print("contracts",contracts.keys(),len(contracts["records"]))
print("public",public.keys(),len(public["feats"]))
print("internal",internal.keys(),len(internal["records"]))
print(json.dumps(contracts["records"][0],indent=2)[:15000])
print("PUBLIC 0\n",json.dumps(public["feats"][0],indent=2)[:14000])
print("REPORTS")
for e in start['evidenceReports']:
 print(Path(e['report']['path']).name, "checks",e.get("checks"),"counts",e.get("counts"),"cohort",e.get("cohort"))
print("FILES", len(manifest))
print("\n".join(n for n in manifest if n.endswith(".py")))

eats, Help and advantage. Preserve the acquisition snapshot. Later prerequisite loss does not erase a grant; ongoing equipped/active/body requirements can disable its use.
NEW nonrepeatability: each identity is acquired once, except the explicitly repeatable ASI, Armor Proficiency for a distinct missing tier, Resilient for a different save, and Weapon Focus for a different actual weapon type. Elemental Affinity/Adept, Spell Focus and other chosen-type feats do not gain unprinted repeatability. One same-named source represented in two catalogs is not two selections. FS-02 Credentials, training snapshots and real study
Use actual current credentials, one proficiency contribution and the feat's selected branch. Thievery is the bounded thieves'-tools/DEX acquisition bridge, not a new universal skill. Mechanical Disable Device means an actual Engineering or thieves'-tools disarm interface; it does not authorize magical disarming. Knowledge (Monstrous Humanoids) remains an existing valid legacy credential; proficient INT(Nature) for that bounded anatomy is the NEW alternative bridge, with printed Survival still valid. Culture Lore may be an actual bounded credential or the explicitly selected History specialization. Agility means the stated Dexterity option, not another ability. Spellcraft is not silently aliased to Arcana; an actual legacy credential receipt is required where it is printed as a prerequisite, unless another printed alternative qualifies. Half-elf, Half-orc, Dragonborn, Sorcerer and Warlock labels are not silently turned into present ancestry/class identities. Existing genuinely authorized legacy grants remain evidenced; no new builder option follows.

**Weapon prerequisite bridge.** Apply this bridge only to an actual printed feat prerequisite, subject to any more specific acquisition direction in that feat’s final procedure. Where a prerequisite names a weapon or weapon type, require actual proficiency with that named current weapon profile/type. A generic Melee or Melee Proficiency prerequisite requires actual proficiency with at least one current melee-weapon profile; Ranged or Ranged Proficiency requires actual proficiency with at least one current ranged-weapon profile. Use the weapon profile’s actual classification, not merely the attack’s melee or ranged delivery: throwing a melee-class weapon does not by itself satisfy Ranged acquisition, and making a ranged spell attack supplies no weapon proficiency. An otherwise generic martial-weapon proficiency prerequisite requires proficiency with at least one actual martial-weapon profile, not all martial weapons; an explicitly stricter per-feat prerequisite remains stricter. Two-Handed or 2H Weapon acquisition requires actual proficiency with a weapon whose current profile possesses the Two-Handed property; proficiency with a Versatile-only weapon held in two hands does not satisfy that gate. Record the qualifying proficiency grant and weapon profile/type in the acquisition snapshot. These are acquisition credentials: they neither supply an item or proficiency nor make an otherwise unproficient attack proficient. They do not impose a new requirement to use the acquisition-qualifying weapon for every later feat event. Actual use-time weapon, proficiency where expressly required, grip, body, range, ammunition, action and other predicates remain those of the exact feat and governing procedure. All other printed ability, level, prerequisite-feat, class-access and earned-opportunity requirements remain mandatory. Class, feat, ancestry and item grants with similar names otherwise remain separate permissions.
Casting-prerequisite snapshot bridge — FEAT327-ACQ-03. NEW acquisition completion. Apply this bridge only to an actual feat acquisition prerequisite or its expressly required acquisition selection, subject to a more specific acquisition rule in that feat’s final procedure. A casting prerequisite requires a source-qualified personal spellcasting entitlement already acquired by the character and still retained at the acquisition snapshot. Record the actual grant, exact spell identity and source version, casting route, relevant class/rank gates, and the repertoire or preparation evidence that makes that route available. Class-level capacity, an RP or RC pool, a spell merely appearing on a permitted list, ownership of a spellbook or scroll, item-enabled casting, an unacquired class feature, and the prospective benefit of the feat being acquired do not by themselves supply this entitlement.

Retained entitlement versus immediate casting legality. A retained personal grant does not lose its acquisition credential merely because its renewable charge is currently empty, RP is insufficient, components or a suitable casting hand/voice are unavailable, the character is unconscious, or an existing temporary prohibition or suppression prevents casting. Ignore those immediate-use impediments only when evaluating this casting credential. Do not refill a resource, restore components, lift a prohibition, perform a cast, or establish that the feat-selection transaction itself is otherwise lawful. Actual casting still requires every current action, component, body, target, range, payment, concentration, strain and source-specific condition. An expressly ongoing or currently-active prerequisite in a particular feat remains an ongoing or currently-active prerequisite.

Qualifying repertoire and preparation. For a known-spell route, require the exact spell actually learned or personally granted, with its applicable source gates satisfied. For an ordinary prepared route, require the spell actually prepared at the snapshot, an actually acquired always-prepared grant, or another already-established source exception that authorizes casting it without ordinary preparation. An unprepared spellbook entry and the ability to prepare or substitute it later are insufficient for that spell’s credential. A future study period, rest, substitution, level-up selection or other repertoire change cannot be anticipated. A ritual route qualifies when the character actually possesses its ritual-casting permission and the particular spell meets that permission’s exact book, tag, rank and other repertoire requirements; no ritual need already have been cast. Do not extend one spell’s ritual overlay to another spell. Loss of a required repertoire source follows the actual class procedure: Wizard’s surviving prepared matrices remain available under its spellbook-loss rule, while an unprepared lost-book entry is not made available by merely planning its reconstruction.

Generic and specific predicates. “Spellcasting ability,” “Actual personal spellcasting ability,” and “Ability to cast at least one spell” require at least one qualifying personal spell route under this bridge; a qualifying rank-0 spell is sufficient. “Ability to cast cantrips” requires a qualifying rank-0 route. A named-spell prerequisite requires the exact required spell identity and permitted source version, not a similarly named class technique or item effect. A school prerequisite requires an actual matching school on the qualifying route. A rank prerequisite uses the spell rank actually supplied by that route and the final normalized predicate’s existing exact-rank or minimum-rank comparison, not total character level, maximum possible class rank, available RP, damage, or a higher price paid for a lower-rank spell. Where one predicate combines identity, school, rank or route restrictions, one qualifying route must satisfy that complete predicate; do not combine different routes’ most favorable fields. Distinct alternatives retain their existing AND/OR structure.

Removal, replacement and expiry. Reevaluate the retained grant and repertoire for every new feat acquisition. An actually removed, expired or lawfully retrained-away grant cannot support a new acquisition unless another independently qualifying route exists. Replacing a prepared matrix removes that ordinary prepared route for later snapshots unless another valid route to the spell remains. Temporary suppression or renewable depletion is not removal. Exhausting a genuinely nonrenewable permission follows its actual source: if the permission terminates, it no longer supplies a retained route. Under FS-01, a previously acquired feat keeps its original acquisition snapshot; later prerequisite loss does not erase that historical acquisition or waive any of the feat’s actual ongoing operating conditions.

Unassigned schools and required school choices. Preserve each source spell’s actual school value, explicit null or absent field. An absent, null or unassigned school is not Universal, Enchantment, Evocation, a wildcard, or a school inferred from damage type or a grant’s name. A school-unassigned personal rank-0 grant can satisfy generic spellcasting and cantrip predicates, but cannot itself satisfy a school-specific predicate. Supplementary Spell Focus additionally requires its mandatory permanent selection of an actual school supported by at least one qualifying acquired spell route at that same snapshot. Satisfying its generic spellcasting prerequisite without a supported school choice is insufficient to complete its acquisition. Another genuine route may support that choice. Subsequent loss or suppression of the supporting route does not reselect the school or erase the original acquisition; later benefits still require actual eligible casts through that fixed school.

Authored-candidate evidence versus runtime selection. For authored-candidate qualification, use the exact complete controlling authored spell procedure and its actual source-access binding, supported by the applicable review and fulfillment chain. A preserved earlier blocked catalog snapshot does not override an explicitly reviewed, version-linked operational descendant merely because the original remains unchanged. A separate selectionEnabled:false or disabled runtime adapter does not by itself invalidate that authored evidence. Neither a favorable label nor the existence of a reviewed spell supplies the character’s personal grant or repertoire history. Genuinely unfinished, null, unsupported or operationally unavailable payloads remain unavailable as positive casting witnesses. Preserve original statuses and live selection flags; this acquisition bridge neither enables runtime selection nor promotes another card.

Snapshot and payment. Preserve the exact prerequisite text and record the qualifying grant, spell/source/mode, actual school-field state and rank, repertoire/preparation or ritual basis, applicable class gates, controlling authored-procedure reference, and acquisition time. Temporary use blocks may be recorded separately but are not treated as grant removal. Missing grant, preparation, source-version or required-school evidence leaves that predicate unestablished rather than assumed true. Check all other printed ability, level, ownership, credential and earned-opportunity requirements independently. The feat’s own prospective spell grant cannot bootstrap its prerequisite, and one paid opportunity cannot fund two acquisitions.

For the six exploration training entries, snapshot each named skill separately at acquisition: grant proficiency if absent; grant the explicitly printed replacing Expertise only if already proficient. An acquired proficiency does not become Expertise merely because proficiency is later acquired elsewhere. If a fixed grant lacks that branch, duplication yields no replacement skill or Expertise. Skill Expert and Prodigy's ordered explicit selections use their own rules. Study, tutoring, language learning, formula research, copying and crafting are different tasks; a bonus or time discount on one does not migrate to another. Apply the actual minimum phase, calendar, rest and physical requirements.

External-credential evidence — FEAT327-ACQ-04. NEW evidence-contract completion; the four existing feat procedures and their prerequisite alternatives are retained. For frenzied_rage, divine_favor, elemental_body and dragon_blood, evaluate a required external credential under ACQ04-EC1 against the same character, world and ordered acquisition snapshot, using the exact operative prerequisite and source version, linked credential or event evidence, and that world's established authority for recording that fact. A source file establishes wording, not that a particular character acquired a feature, worships a deity, experienced an event or possesses a lineage. A name, nonempty story label, unchecked Boolean, prospective feat benefit or unrelated class/ancestry/item grant supplies no credential. The supporting grant, worship state or exposure event must already qualify before acquisition; same-time events require an authoritative earlier sequence. Worship must be established at that snapshot; exposure is a qualifying prior event, not a requirement to remain exposed. Apply the branch-specific facts and required choices in ACQ04-EC1 without adding doctrine, an exposure threshold, an element-to-plane matching rule or a legacy alias. A conditional, opaque test-world fixture can establish only a CONDITIONAL_ONLY predicate result within that fixture. It supplies no actual campaign history, selectable option, class feature, cosmology approval or entitlement. Missing, unbound, mismatched or insufficient evidence leaves a predicate UNESTABLISHED; affirmative incompatible evidence is CONTRADICTED. Neither result satisfies a required AND node, although a separately satisfied printed OR alternative remains valid. An ESTABLISHED_FOR_SNAPSHOT result requires independently admitted actual-world evidence and is not supplied by this review. Preserve all original source fields, absence/null distinctions, acquisition history and current status/selection flags. All other permanent-ability, level, parent-feat, mandatory-choice and earned-opportunity predicates remain independently required; one paid opportunity cannot fund two acquisitions. Later use still requires the exact feat's ongoing predicates. This evidence policy does not reopen ACQ-03 or change its casting-snapshot rules.

FS-03 Distinct feat bonuses and retained comparison families
NEW joint convention: after eligibility, flat numeric bonuses from DISTINCT feat identities add unless an explicit shared comparison family says otherwise. One feat identity contributes once to the same event/statistic across duplicate representations, sources and owners. This replaces Extended Magic's blanket strongest-same-stat proposal and the conflicting individual statements/examples named in the correction register. It does not rewrite the eight reviewed feats or reviewed maximum-HP components.
Class techniques still perform their own strongest-class-bonus comparison; spells perform their reviewed same-stat comparison; item, attire, tool, facility, cover and item-enhancement contributions retain their own reviewed families. Then add the independently legal feat contribution. A feat-created weapon/armor enhancement explicitly put into an equipment family remains there, not also a feat bonus. Same named condition penalties retain their own comparison rather than becoming additive because a feat created them. Advantage/disadvantage still cancel normally; more sources of advantage do not outvote one disadvantage. Damage resistance of the same scope applies once; immunity retains priority; keep the greatest remaining temporary HP, not their sum. Expanded critical ranges use the broadest applicable range rather than adding chance points. Copies cannot exploit a new family name to duplicate a source. Actual unusual source overrides retain their explicitly narrower predicates.

FS-04 Native damage, percentages and hostile slows
NEW percentage convention: each separately eligible percentage feat computes its own floored increment from the SAME native packet, after actual critical-dice adjustment and its ordinary native ability/flat formula but BEFORE external riders, other percentage increments, saves and defenses. Add the increments; never multiply them together or floor a combined percentage instead of the separate increments. A feat's exact eligible base remains controlling: Anatomy Lore and Precision Strike do not acquire a giant-slayer multiplier on unrelated spells, for example. Added external Smite/sneak/copy/retaliation/rider packets do not enter another feat's native base unless expressly named. Apply a shared-area roll's increment once to that shared roll, then each actual target's save/defenses. For a mixed native packet requiring division, compute the increment once, distribute proportional to nonnegative native typed amounts by largest remainder with original component order breaking ties, and preserve integer totals. Do not add the full increment to each type. Additional dice are added before actual critical doubling and save fractions; a maximized die is not an actually rolled face for rolled-face triggers.
KEEP hostile slow arbitration: evaluate each adverse Speed effect against the same pre-control Speed and take the lowest surviving result; do not add Slasher and Crippling Strike's two -10 penalties, or subtract -10 again after a stronger half-Speed. Distinct records retain their own deadlines and suppression. Root/physical restraint, missing movement modes, terrain costs, burden, systemic Strain and actual Wounds retain their proper independent stages. No movement already spent is refunded. Percent damage addition is not the rule for percentage work/cost/consumption reductions: those use their specifically declared strongest-factor family.

FS-05 Roll type, reroll window and targets
KEEP attacks versus AC, binary saves, fixed-DC Core tasks with natural shifts, and opposed totals with unchanged-state ties lost by the initiator. A +10 attack margin is not a critical. Natural 1/20 on attacks retain their attack rules; saves do not acquire task shifts. A failed-save reroll replaces the whole original one- or two-d20 procedure with the same actual modifiers and advantage/disadvantage, before consequences, unless the exact feat instead replaces a named die position. Use the replacement even if worse; a source cannot reroll its own replacement. Lucky and Elven Accuracy keep their separately specified die-position rules. Costs and prior consequences are not rolled back.
KEEP the current class target convention: 'ally' includes the owner unless the source says OTHER, or real geometry/activity inherently requires a different creature. Do not count the same person twice in 'you and an ally', a team, a flanking pair or an assistance role. 'Enemy' requires an actual current opponent, not every creature in range; meaningful-hostility and anti-farm gates apply only where the source/this register binds resource or encounter rewards. Do not add an enemy or anatomy gate to an unrelated ordinary attack merely as a blanket convention. Source perception, actual reach/line of effect, body eligibility and willing/understood channels remain real.

### chef | Chef | reviewed
PRINTED prerequisite: "None"
REPEAT {"policy": "nonrepeatable", "permanentChoice": true}
## Chef — General; no prerequisite

During an actual long-rest attempt, use cook's utensils and spend one uninterrupted hour cooking. This is permitted light activity only when the normal long-rest procedure permits that hour; the feat does not enlarge the allowance. Designate one batch of1..current PB intended servings at cooking start. Only one batch may be designated per rest attempt and only one can gain potency per successful completed rest.

On successful cooking completion, consume one real standard ration or meal-equivalent ingredient per intended serving. Create exactly that many physical prepared-food items, each with its own identity. Their food exists immediately, but they gain no feat potency before the rest actually succeeds. If the rest fails, the ingredients remain spent and the food remains ordinary. An incomplete cooking hour creates no special food.

At successful long-rest completion, first resolve old rest-expiring effects and advance the rest epoch. Then snapshot the Chef's current PB and empower up to `min(physically prepared servings still present, current PB)` items. Each grants `2 × PB at batch finalization` temporary HP. A PB increase cannot manufacture more ingredients or servings; a decrease leaves excess prepared items ordinary.

A creature with a legal serving must spend one uninterrupted minute eating it. Completing a legal offer consumes the serving and the recipient's **Chef-family entitlement for its current completed-long-rest epoch**, even if an equal/stronger existing grant wins. This limit is shared across every Chef. The Chef's own rest does not reset another recipient's entitlement.

Potency ends at the earlier of24 elapsed hours from successful batch creation or the Chef's next completed long rest after the creation rest. An already consumed/discarded item never returns. Expired potency leaves ordinary food, with no magical premium value. Potency expiration does not end a previously granted shield; that shield has the recipient's default lifetime.

Chef servings are distinct from crafted Feast recipes. Chef grants no recipe ingredients, crafting checks, RP restoration, creature traits or recipe power. It does not occupy the recipe system's one-feast-at-a-time slot. A lawful recipe rider can coexist with a Chef rider; temporary HP still do not stack. One physical meal cannot claim both effects unless an exact recipe expressly supports it. Recipe-specific ten-minute eating, longer crafting and materials remain independent.

**NEW:** the finite batch transaction, quantities, preparation/eating times, recipient-family cap, potency expiry and explicit no-duplication/economy bridges. The source's long-rest cooking role is preserved.


### empowered_summoning | Empowered Summoning | reviewed
PRINTED prerequisite: "Charisma 14+"
REPEAT {"policy": "nonrepeatable", "permanentChoice": true}
## Empowered Summoning — Magic; Charisma14+

When your actual source activation genuinely creates a new finite summoned creature, offer it `2 × your PB at creation` temporary HP. Its lifetime is default recipient long rest, also ending when that creature instance ends. Returned/revived preexisting bodies, persistent companion redeploy/rebuild, Mage bound returns, Ranger bonds and devices do not qualify merely by appearing again.

Record a lineage key containing the owner, actual source activation root and creation-slot/body-lineage. Several distinct bodies created by one source each receive their own grant. Reissuing/recreating the same lineage never refreshes its creation grant or its round damage entitlement. A genuinely new activation paid/used under its own rules can create a new root. An Action, limited use, ritual use or item charge can be a real source cost; RP is not mandatory.

Each qualifying creature adds its owner's **current PB** to its first actual damage roll in each canonical round/window. Add this flat bonus once to a shared area roll, not once per affected target. A roll on an ongoing damage tick can qualify; fixed damage without a roll does not consume or receive the rider. Later rolls in that window gain nothing. A critical hit does not double this flat PB bonus.

The creature remains attacker and damage owner. This is not the owner's spell damage. All existing companion command, Bonus Action and offensive budgets remain unchanged. The feat grants neither an initiative entry nor an independent command budget.

**NEW integration:** exact lineage identity, recreation guard and preserved round phase. Creation amount and damage rider are tied to their exact respective PB timings.

## Inspiring Leader — General; Charisma13+

Speak for ten uninterrupted minutes. At the start, select up to your current PB **other** willing creatures within30ft. Snapshot your character level and actual signed Charisma modifier then. ### lifeleech | Lifeleech | reviewed

## Lifeleech — Magic; actual spellcasting ability

Before your spell's damage resolves, designate one eligible hostile target for that spell root and canonical round/window. Your first positive actual necrotic damage to that target in the window commits the entitlement, even if the eventual healing is0, prohibited, capped or unfunded. No retargeting after observing results; area spells do not harvest a separate heal from every target. The same continuing spell can qualify again in a later real round/window.

Raw self-healing is `floor(actual necrotic damage dealt /2)`. Targets must be genuine hostile opposition, not friendly/staged/trivial sacrifices or owner/ally-generated farming bodies. **NEW finite resource — Lifeleech Recovery Reserve:** newly acquiring the feat starts at0. All lawful healing bonuses consume this same reserve. Then restore HP subject to ordinary maximum HP and Critical ceiling, and calculate `genuine excess = max(0, funded amount − ordinary missing HP)`.

A broad HP-gain prohibition produces0 legal amount, spends0 reserve and creates0 excess. At reserve0 the round/root trigger still commits, but it produces neither restoration nor excess. Full HP can produce funded excess, but does not enable conditional bonuses requiring actual HP restoration. Critical's ceiling does not itself turn all unrestored points into excess.

Lifeleech provides no intrinsic excess converter. A separately authorized converter must have its own eligibility/cost/cadence/cap and consume each funded excess point once. An explicitly legal resource-output converter remains possible; incidental heal-triggered resource production is blocked. No crystal is required for Lifeleech's own collection. Use the shared resource/death rules below. Lifeleech alone cannot spend charges through abilities from the unowned Soul Cage feat.


### necrotic_affinity | Necrotic Affinity | reviewed

## Necrotic Affinity — Magic; Religion proficiency **or** character level8+

You have resistance to necrotic damage. Once per actual turn, when you hit with a melee attack, you may add1d6 necrotic damage. Natural/form melee attacks and off-turn Reaction attacks can qualify. A critical hit doubles this die to2d6; extra Actions in the same turn do not refresh it, while a genuinely different extra turn has its own entitlement. The die is feat damage, not spell damage.

Preserve the previously reviewed absorption procedure: when a legal necrotic spell/supernatural packet reaches the post-passive-defense stage, you may pay your Reaction to prevent `floor(post-defense necrotic damage /2)` before the old temporary-HP buffer. Afterwards, if the holder remains living, offer temporary HP equal to the prevented amount until the end of the holder's next original regular turn, using its real endpoint marker. This includes your spells, the feat die, Agony Lance or another genuinely personal necrotic source. It is not limited to the feat's1d6. Supplying a summoned/transformed creature's statistics does not make its damage yours.

A mixed-type fatal root qualifies if your positive resolved necrotic component belongs to that root and the root actually causes death; no counterfactual subtraction test. Direct Instant Death, fatal death failures at0HP, or a later consequence expressly preserving that necrotic causal root can qualify. Temporary-HP-only absorption with no harmful consequence does not establish a seal.

The seal blocks applicable resurrection with an **actual spellRank/effectiveRank0..5**. Never invent a rank for unranked class features. Reviewed Paladin Grace Reversal is rank5 and is blocked; ordinary Resurrection7 and True Resurrection9 are above this restriction. Other resurrection requirements and Soul Bind still apply independently.

Same owner/death instance does not duplicate; different owners can have separate endpoints, but boolean blocking does not compound. A seal ends by elapsed expiry or an exact source able to remove that blocker, not generic cure. A legal return to life closes that death instance; its historical seals do not persist onto a later death. Transformations never create a new soul/body identity to evade a seal.

**NEW integration:** actual-turn identity, rank translation and fatal-root/death-instance bookkeeping. The broader printed necrotic-kill scope is preserved, correcting the narrower first draft.

PRINTED prerequisite: "Lifeleech or spellcasting ability, level 8+"
REPEAT {"policy": "nonrepeatable", "permanentChoice": true}
## Soul Cage feat — Magic; level8+ and (Lifeleech **or** actual spellcasting ability)

On acquisition, lock one actually possessed spellcasting source/ability as this feat's DC provider. Agony uses that source's current normal spell-save DC. No freely switchable DC ability or general retraining is created.

**Shared Soul Charges:** each owner has one persistent pool0..3, shared with Lifeleech. A charge is a soul **spark/residue**, not custody of the whole soul. It neither prevents resurrection nor replaces/removes Soul Bind. The ordinary Soul Cage spell is a separate rank6/13RP spell awaiting its own operational review. Soulrender's separate whole-soul ritual unlock still requires actually binding the soul of a living named enemy; residue cannot satisfy it.

At an eligible hostile soul-bearing creature's actual death within30ft, with perception and legal line of effect, Soul Cage permits immediate optional capture with no Action or Reaction. A discrete physical black crystal must actually be possessed and available. Lifeleech collection is automatic and needs no crystal. Owning both never gives two charges for the same death.

Each owner permanently records the exact death event as evaluated, including ineligible events. There is no later corpse rescan after moving closer, gaining a feat/crystal or improving perception. For an eligible event, also reserve the immutable body for that owner's current completed-long-rest epoch. Declining or being at full capacity consumes that entitlement; there is no latent fourth charge. A new real death after resurrection is a new event, but the same body cannot supply the owner again in the same epoch. A future completed rest can permit a future new death, never the old corpse. Different owners evaluate independently.

Created/generated farming bodies and immutable object-origin transformed creatures are ineligible. Soul status otherwise follows actual source/canon or Director adjudication; this feat does not declare every undead/construct category soulless. Death/root/body identities persist through forms.

Charges live on the owner and do not decay automatically. Losing/destroying/transferring the crystal transfers or erases **zero** charges; Soul Cage capture/spending is unavailable without another actual legal black crystal. Another feat owner can use the transferred physical crystal for their own pool. There is no invented price, attunement, HP, durability or crystal charge capacity. During one uninterrupted quiet minute handling the crystal, you may voluntarily dissipate owned charges; this does nothing to any deceased soul.

**Shield:** Bonus Action; pay1 Soul Charge; one legal ally within30ft, including yourself; roll2d8 temporary HP with default lifetime. Pay before strongest-only comparison. An equal/stronger old shield does not refund the charge or Bonus Action.

**Agony Lance:** Action; pay2 Soul Charges; one perceived legal creature within60ft; CON save against the locked provider's current DC. Deal3d8 necrotic damage, half rounded down on success, then ordinary defenses/Core apply once. This is feat damage owned by you, not a spell, RP expenditure or spellcasting trigger.

The target also gains one **Agony healing-suppression family** record until the next committed ordinary HP-healing event with positive pre-reduction potential, or one elapsed minute. That next healing receives an exact×1/2 factor in the canonical healing pipeline. Save success or necrotic immunity affects damage but does not remove this separate rider. Multiple Agony Lances never multiply to1/4: a new paid application refreshes the single endpoint to `max(old expiry, new application+60s)` and retains contributor history.

Consume the suppression on the first applicable committed healing event even if a stronger arithmetic branch wins or actual healing is later capped/prohibited. Suppressed/inapplicable records do not apply or consume; absolute time continues. Temporary HP, stabilization, resurrection, prevention and maximum-HP changes are not ordinary HP healing and do not consume it. Removal requires an exact source capable of removing this magical suppression/debuff; generic cure is insufficient.

**NEW:** prerequisite parsing, residue identity, shared resource/death transaction, conduit role, activations/ranges/DC and the exact healing-suppression conversion. Original source IDs and relationships remain distinct.

A qualifying completion after the event is sufficient; no additional rule requires the whole rest to have started after it. Existing active-work/rest restrictions still apply. A dead animal never becomes eligible merely through rest, release, purchase or this procedure. Voluntary release requires the owner's one uninterrupted hour of actual release work. End the outgoing bond's control entitlement and unexecuted commands; preserve all already-spent body/actor allowances. No automatic transfer of custody or command occurs. Record this end for the post-ending Long-Rest gate. A creature cannot simultaneously have this and a Ranger bond, or two owners' Animal Companion bonds. NEW ACQ-05 correction of 'new paid acquisition': a transfer requires the outgoing bond to end through its actual lawful release/death/termination procedure, real lawful access/custody for the receiver, and the receiver's independently authorized new bond. An existing Animal Companion feat owner does not buy that nonrepeatable feat again; they spend their own available attempt and full hour and satisfy their own post-ending Long-Rest gate, if any. The sender's rest, feat ownership or spent attempt neither transfers nor resets the receiver's history. A receiver without this feat must first acquire it through their own actual eligible unspent opportunity, or use an independently acquired receiving engine; receiving the animal grants no feat or class feature. No universal transfer payment, new animal, healing, additive chassis, second controller, extra phase or refunded body/resource budget is granted. Owner death ends the bond and, if the animal remains alive, restores its saved ordinary profile using the same positive-HP ratio conversion as release; a dead animal remains its actual corpse. Temporary owner incapacity prevents new commands but does not cancel a previously committed legal command; after that command, uncommanded phases remain defensive. This never supplies autonomous attacks.

# Direct source navigation; output each exact matching record instead of unbounded envelopes.
equip=load(base+'data/playtest/equipment_source_catalog.json')
eqcurrent=load(base+'data/playtest/equipment_acquisition_candidate.json')
foundation=load(base+'data/playtest/character_foundation.json')
ancestry=load(base+'data/playtest/ancestry_background_candidate.json')
monk=load(base+'data/public/class_playtests/monk.json')
print('schemas', {k:list(v)[:20] for k,v in [('equip',equip),('equipment',eqcurrent),('foundation',foundation),('ancestry',ancestry)]})
def find_dicts(obj,pred,path=""):
 if isinstance(obj,dict):
  if pred(obj): yield path,obj
  for k,v in obj.items(): yield from find_dicts(v,pred,path+'/'+str(k).replace('~','~0').replace('/','~1'))
 elif isinstance(obj,list):
  for i,v in enumerate(obj): yield from find_dicts(v,pred,path+'/'+str(i))
for label,obj in [('equip',equip),('current',eqcurrent),('ancestry',ancestry)]:
 for ptr,o in find_dicts(obj,lambda x:x.get('id') in ['simple-melee-weapons.unarmed-strike','troll--claws']):
  print(label,ptr,json.dumps(o,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
print('MONK ENGINE')
for i,s in enumerate(monk['engine']):
 if any(t in s.lower() for t in ['unarmed','proficien','weapon','martial arts']):
  print('/engine/'+str(i),s)
for ptr,o in find_dicts(foundation,lambda x:x.get('name')=='Monk' or x.get('className')=='Monk'):
 print('FOUNDATION',ptr,json.dumps(o,ensure_ascii=False)[:7000])
txt=(R/(base+'docs/mechanics/EQUIPMENT_ACQUISITION_PLAYTEST.md')).read_text()
for n,line in enumerate(txt.splitlines(),1):
 if 'unarmed' in line.lower(): print('EQGUIDE',n,line)
txt=(R/(base+'docs/mechanics/ANCESTRY_BACKGROUND_PLAYTEST.md')).read_text()
for n,line in enumerate(txt.splitlines(),1):
 if any(s in line.lower() for s in ['natural weapon','claws','proficien']): print('ANCGUIDE',n,line)

MONK ENGINE
/engine/0 PROVISIONAL PLAYTEST CONTRACT. Dexterity is the Monk class ability. S = 1/2/3/4 at Monk levels 1/5/11/17. M = max(1, Dexterity modifier). Monk technique attack = d20 + normal character proficiency bonus + Dexterity modifier. Monk technique save DC = 8 + normal character proficiency bonus + Dexterity modifier.
/engine/5 Tiger, Tortoise and Crane Ki generation obey the global Ki-gain ceilings. Tiger can generate Ki from critical unarmed strikes. Tortoise can generate Ki from specified defensive successes. Crane provides mobility and support but does not create free out-of-combat healing. A single event can satisfy more than one generator, but total gain still cannot exceed the appropriate own-turn or off-turn ceiling.
/engine/15 All acquisition and scaling gates use Monk class level, not total character level. Normal character proficiency bonus still uses the game's ordinary total-character-level rule. A multiclass dip cannot unlock Master, Tortoise Final Form, Hero features or a level-20 Final.
/engine/17 Unarmored Flow completion: while wearing no armor or shield, AC=10+DEX modifier+WIS modifier, or ordinary armorless AC if higher, never both. Unarmed strikes use DEX for attack/damage and 1d6 at 1, 1d8 at 5, 1d10 at 11, 1d12 at 17. This is a new numeric completion; no automatic Extra Attack beyond printed techniques.
EQGUIDE 11 The source catalog retains unknown fields as null. Its printed price or weight can apply to a bundle, such as twenty arrows, rather than one unit. The operational projection records bundle size and unit values separately. Unarmed strike is an attack option, not an inventory object. Services and living mounts are not generic mundane objects with a weapon's durability.
ANCGUIDE 15 Ancestry “level” and proficiency bonus use total character level. Class gates still use the actual class level. Ogre is Large; Troll is Medium. Powerful Build affects carrying capacity and push/drag/lift weight only, never size category, space, weapon damage, grappling eligibility or reach. Apply the reviewed body/equipment rules to real Large geometry and fit.
ANCGUIDE 17 Use the foundation's duplicate-proficiency procedure. A choice remains within its actual list; a fixed ancestry/background skill uses its stated alternative list, or any of the 20 basic skills if none is printed. Tool choices stay within the permitted list/category. If every legal choice is already owned, that grant adds nothing and does not block creation or advancement. No duplicate creates Expertise, another category, a feat, or a future selection. Weapon/armor/save duplicates have no compensation.
ANCGUIDE 19 An explicit “twice your proficiency bonus instead” trait supplies 2 × PB only for its described task, even if otherwise untrained; never PB plus another 2 × PB. Mere advantage grants no proficiency. Identical resistances do not stack. A natural armor formula is one available AC formula; it is not added to worn armor. Explicit flat AC bonuses remain bonuses subject to ordinary stacking.
ANCGUIDE 21 The package/wealth decision in the foundation remains controlling: rolled wealth replaces both class and background physical gear and package coins, but not proficiencies, languages, features or standings. Possession grants no proficiency. An Academy spellbook/focus grants no spells; Underworld Escapee's disguise kit grants no disguise proficiency. A journal, signet, map, relic or token has no invented magical effect, price or item statistic beyond its source.
ANCGUIDE 67 Forge Dwarf Master Craftsman reduces actual artisan-tool creation time by 25%. “Exceptional quality” is retained as a quality label without an invented price/stat bonus; its wider crafting effect remains open. Stonecunning and other specific double-PB checks follow the task-scoped proficiency rule above. Deep Dwarf's 120-foot darkvision replaces, rather than adds to, 60 feet.
ANCGUIDE 89 Scrap Goblin uses actual suitable available materials to create one listed functional item; its HP is half ordinary item HP, minimum 1. Using the trait again ends the previous item. A healing kit requires actual medicinal consumables sufficient for its normal finite uses; reusing exhausted scraps cannot restore them. It grants no proficiency, magic item or arbitrary free consumable. Other simple tools require the printed Director agreement. Missing equipment profiles remain visible dependencies.
ANCGUIDE 163 | Dimensional Refugee | History (original world) is a scoped proficiency: add PB only on History checks materially about that world. # Animal Companion lifecycle — ACQ05 finite acceptance

206 checks pass across20 named groups and71 recorded conditional traces. Purchase, spent start and completion/interruption are distinct ordered events. Release/replacement use the existing feat, and transfer follows the receiving owner’s own history. Four HP endpoints remain4/1/3/6 at maximum9; growth does not heal.

- All animal, relationship, custody, work, rest, prior Ranger selection and class-level attainment facts are explicit conditional premises, not authenticated campaign records.

model ownership headers
1:"""Bounded paid ownership chains, using source-derived training and real ASIs.
2:
3:This models acquisition prerequisites and the named selection/initialization
4:fields only. It is not a class builder, combat engine or proof of campaign awards.
5:"""
6:from copy import deepcopy
7:from feat_exact_01_acquisition_models import PaidChoices, VisionChoice
8:from feat_exact_01_training_prerequisite_model import materialize_training, meets_training_prerequisite
9:
10:TARGETS = {
11:    'alertness_improved','athlete_improved','climber_improved','martial_adept_advanced',
12:    'giant_slayer','life_drinker','precision_strike','quickened_thought','slayer_s_resolve','undying_flesh',
13:}
14:PARENTS = {
15:    'alert','alertness','athlete','climber','martialAdept','dark_affinity','hemocraft',
16:    'weaponFocus','arcane_calculus','temporal_theory','favored_enemy_dragon','necrotic_affinity',
17:    'dark_fortitude','abilityScoreImprovement',
18:}
19:
20:class OwnershipChain:
21:    def __init__(self, actor, classes, equipment, skill_abilities, ancestry, backgrounds, contracts, maneuvers):
22:        self.actor=deepcopy(actor)
23:        if actor['priorFeatChoices']:
24:            raise ValueError('All prior feats must be earned and bought through this history')
25:        self.first=actor['firstClass']
26:        self.classes=classes;self.equipment=equipment;self.skills=skill_abilities
27:        self.ancestry=ancestry;self.backgrounds=backgrounds;self.contracts=contracts
28:        self.maneuvers=set(maneuvers)
29:        initial=materialize_training(actor,classes,equipment,skill_abilities,ancestry,backgrounds)
30:        self.pay=PaidChoices({name:row['asiClassLevels'] for name,row in classes.items()}, {},
31:                            deepcopy(actor['classLevels']),deepcopy(initial['scores']),set(contracts),
32:                            weapon_proficiencies=set(initial['weapons']))
33:        self.initializations={}
34:
35:    def snapshot(self):
36:        return {'actor':deepcopy(self.actor),'payment':deepcopy(self.pay.__dict__),
37:                'initializations':deepcopy(self.initializations)}
38:
39:    def training(self):
40:        result=materialize_training(self.actor,self.classes,self.equipment,self.skills,self.ancestry,self.backgrounds)
41:        # Permanent increases are recorded in the paid history; they are not new rolls.
42:        result['scores']=deepcopy(self.pay.scores)
43:        return result
44:
45:    def advance(self,level):
46:        old=self.actor['classLevels'][self.first]
47:        if type(level) is not int or not old<=level<=20:
48:            raise ValueError('History must advance through attained legal class levels')
49:        self.actor['classLevels']={self.first:level};self.pay.current_classes={self.first:level}
50:        for sid in ['hemocraft','life_drinker']:
51:            if sid in self.initializations:
52:                reserve=self.initializations[sid]['recoveryReserve']
53:                reserve['maximum']=2*level
54:                reserve['current']=min(reserve['current'],reserve['maximum'])
55:
56:    def earn(self,class_level):
57:        self.pay.earn_class_opportunity(self.first+':'+str(class_level),self.first,class_level)
58:
59:    def prerequisite(self,sid,choice=None):
60:        t=self.training();actor=deepcopy(self.actor)
61:        if sid=='weaponFocus':actor['weaponChoice']=choice
62:        def evaluate(node):
63:            if node['kind']=='ownedFeat':
64:                return self.pay.has(node['id'])
65:            if node['kind']=='all':
66:                return bool(node['requirements']) and all(evaluate(x) for x in node['requirements'])
67:            if node['kind']=='any':
68:                return bool(node['requirements']) and any(evaluate(x) for x in node['requirements'])
69:            return meets_training_prerequisite(node,actor,t,self.equipment,self.skills)
70:        return evaluate(self.contracts[sid]['requirements'])
71:
72:    def acquire(self,sid,at_class_level,choice=None):
73:        if sid not in TARGETS|PARENTS:
74:            raise ValueError('Identity outside this finite owned-feat cohort')
75:        qualified=self.prerequisite(sid,choice)
76:        if not qualified:
77:            raise ValueError('Actual source prerequisite not established by the paid history')
78:        additions={}
79:        if sid=='athlete':
80:            if choice not in {'STR','DEX'}:
81:                raise ValueError('Athlete requires a permanent Strength or Dexterity choice')
82:            additions={'ability':choice,'increase':max(0,min(1,20-self.pay.scores[choice]))}
83:        if sid in {'martialAdept','martial_adept_advanced'}:
84:            if not isinstance(choice,list) or len(choice)!=2 or len(set(choice))!=2 or not set(choice)<=self.maneuvers:
85:                raise ValueError('Choose two different entries from the actual six-maneuver menu')
86:            if sid=='martial_adept_advanced':
87:                known=self.initializations['martialAdept']['maneuverIds']
88:                if set(choice)&set(known):
89:                    raise ValueError('Advanced choices must be two additional maneuvers')
90:            additions={'maneuverIds':deepcopy(choice)}
91:        if sid=='dark_affinity':
92:            # These witnesses supply no independent darkvision source; no class-card grants are inferred.
93:            if choice!={'independentDarkvisionRanges':[]}:
94:                raise ValueError('This source slice establishes only the no-independent-darkvision branch')
95:            additions={'darkvisionBranch':VisionChoice.acquire([]).branch,'teleportUses':{'maximum':1,'current':1}}
96:        if sid=='hemocraft':
97:            if choice not in {'INT','CHA'}:
98:                raise ValueError('Hemocraft requires its permanent Intelligence or Charisma DC choice')
99:            additions={'governingAbility':choice,'recoveryReserve':{'maximum':2*self.training()['totalLevel'],'current':0}}
100:        if sid=='life_drinker':
101:            additions={'recoveryReserve':{'maximum':2*self.training()['totalLevel'],'current':2*self.training()['totalLevel']}}
102:        # PaidChoices validates real opportunities, identity/repetition and Weapon Focus/ASI choices atomically.
103:        record=self.pay.commit(sid,self.first+':'+str(at_class_level),choice=choice,prerequisites_met=qualified)
104:        if sid=='athlete':self.pay.scores[choice]+=additions['increase']
105:        if sid=='martialAdept':additions['superiorityPool']={'die':'d6','maximum':1,'current':1}
106:        if sid=='martial_adept_advanced':
107:            parent=self.initializations['martialAdept']
108:            parent['maneuverIds'].extend(choice)
109:            parent['superiorityPool']['maximum']+=1
110:            parent['superiorityPool']['current']+=1
111:            additions['sharedPoolOwner']='martialAdept'
112:        if additions:self.initializations[sid]=additions
113:        return record

conjure_flora Once per beneficial SR/LR use an Action to create one of the three complete NEW seedling profiles below on visible supported ground within 30 ft and line of effect. It lasts one minute on original source-start markers, needs no concentration, and is a magical nonspell conjured body. Capacity is 1, or 2 at total level 10. NEW to make the second-capacity clause usable, at level 10 this same committed use permits one additional Action before the first plant expires to create one second plant; no second rest use, no later replacement of that spent creation. This is an explicit proposed addition to the printed one-seedling creation, not a recovered rule. Each plant gets its own minute. Choose its mode/burst type at creation. One BA may command all active plants within 60 ft able to perceive you, but produces only ONE selected mode outcome per round under the shared offensive-companion allowance. Lash chooses one creature within 10 ft of any participating plant for 1d6 piercing, DEX save half. Spore and entangle use their full profiles. No autonomous turn, opportunity attacks, harvest, loot, Supply, corpse or resource-generating death. Destruction/expiry permanently consumes the body.
NEW ordinary command scheduling: each plant has one attached phase immediately after its owner’s regular turn, not an independent initiative turn. Its ordinary commanded action requires the owner’s shared Bonus Action within60ft through the specified perceivable command channel and spends the one offensive companion allowance per owner per numbered round if offensive. With no legal command it has no autonomous attack. Trapcasting’s explicitly adopted entry-trigger or burst is a separate narrow exception spending that same shared offensive allowance, never another free attack.
arcane_adaptation After an actual successful legal reflection or capture of a spell/supernatural effect through a separately owned source, gain resistance to the one real damage type expressed by its selected copied/reflected component until your next future regular end. NEW choose one observed type for a mixed packet; nondamaging effects grant no damage resistance. It begins after capture/redirect, does not retroactively mitigate earlier damage, and grants no copying permission by itself. Once SR/LR at that event, also mark the one legal captured profile as temporarily known through your second future regular start. During that interval you may activate that exact bounded profile using its actual action, RP, components, rank ceiling and concentration, even if its original one-use record was consumed; no other original spell/monster ability is learned. Each activation is a copy retaining the original no-proc/no-copy/resource flags and the shared one-copy-release/round cap below. Existing Mirrorsage records retain their actual release costs/caps and printed bounded profile. No restoration of archive slots or legacy boss power. Expiry removes permission but not legitimately established effects before their own deadlines.
eldritch_echo Require character 3 AND (Forbidden Pact OR actual spellcasting). Gain the exact NEW Eldritch Blast cantrip profile below once; duplicate grant through Forbidden Pact shares one identity. Once SR/LR when you actually perceive a creature within 60 ft/line of effect completing an eligible noncopyspell, use a Reaction to capture one matching bounded copy profile below. NEW the source may be ally or foe, but cannot be a resource/copy/production effect or protected boss/legendary ability. Record original rank and the profile’s half-rankq; it must fit your actual qualifying casting route’s learnable ceiling, with Forbidden Pact’s explicit rank 0 grant supplying no ranked ceiling by itself. On your next future regular turn use anAction, actualRPprice and V/S and any additional actual copied-profile component requirement to release that one profile; otherwise it expires at that turn’s end. freq=[]

 if clauses:freq.append((sid,clauses))
for starti in range(0,len(freq),35):
